# Catálogo de commits de `development`

Este documento conserva el título y la descripción registrados en Git para cada commit alcanzable desde la rama `development`. No interpreta los diffs ni inventa descripciones ausentes.

## Alcance e inventario

- Rama inventariada: `development`
- Revisión HEAD inventariada: `2bb1cb117c4e7fd36f96e31719ab97bbb34337fc`
- Total de commits: **1,778**
- Commits con descripción adicional: **627**
- Commits sin descripción adicional: **1,151**
- Orden: cronológico, del commit más antiguo al más reciente.
- Incluye commits funcionales, correcciones, pruebas, infraestructura, documentación, merges, reversiones y cambios temporales.

## 0081. `5a9ed6114` — Fix enum constants, constructor initialization, and native string joins

- Commit: `5a9ed611416cfce39711efc267ce246475af92dd`
- Fecha: 2026-09-08
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Fix enum constants, constructor initialization, and native string joins
>
> Support compile-time enum member expressions, including bitwise combinations of preceding members and shift-based values, while preserving overflow and underlying-type validation.
>
> Allow simple assignments to get-only auto-properties inside their declaring instance constructor by targeting the generated backing field without synthesizing a setter. Keep assignments from ordinary methods and through other instances rejected.
>
> Allow equality and inequality comparisons between enums and the integer literal zero. Materialize the enum conversion during HIR lowering so enums with non-int underlying types remain verifier-correct, while nonzero integer comparisons remain invalid.
>
> Use the existing implicit string-literal-to-native-string conversion when one conditional-expression branch is a string literal and the other is a builtin string. Preserve lazy evaluation and continue rejecting unrelated branch types.
>
> Add focused semantic and negative tests, O0/O2 execution coverage, NSLink coverage, and corresponding language-specification and conformance updates.

---

## 0082. `4b9ac39af` — Documenta la unión condicional de strings nativos / Document native string conditional joins

- Commit: `4b9ac39afdfcd7fac83947939078619e03d1cf46`
- Fecha: 2026-09-08
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Registra el defecto, la regla estrecha entre string-literal y builtin:string, los archivos y funciones modificados, la decisión de no introducir una unión general al estilo C# y la evidencia enfocada del cambio incluido en 5a9ed611.
>
> EN: Record the defect, the narrow string-literal/builtin:string rule, the modified files and functions, the decision not to introduce a general C#-style join, and the focused evidence for the change included in 5a9ed611.

---

## 0083. `3e5637208` — Corrige literales positivos que requieren ulong / Fix positive literals that require ulong

- Commit: `3e56372089fdb134982b175d47ba227b63776f14`
- Fecha: 2026-09-08
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Tipa como ulong los literales positivos sin sufijo que exceden long.MaxValue, conserva la regla especial de los mínimos negativos y agrega cobertura directa para ulong.MaxValue. Restaura la ejecución de enums de 64 bits y el diagnóstico de que no existe sucesor después del máximo.
>
> EN: Type unsuffixed positive literals above long.MaxValue as ulong, preserve the special rule for negative minima, and add direct ulong.MaxValue coverage. Restore 64-bit enum execution and the no-successor diagnostic after the maximum value.

---

## 0084. `50ec8a3b3` — Permite préstamos temporales de string en constructores / Allow temporary string borrows in constructors

- Commit: `50ec8a3b328ec77a172e06c872f36fab4cbd50d8`
- Fecha: 2026-09-08
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Aplica a new, this y base la misma regla de préstamo de expresión completa usada por las llamadas ordinarias. Mantiene vivos los strings owned frescos mediante un owner oculto, permite literales con duración de proceso y conserva el rechazo de escapes desde parámetros borrowed.
>
> EN: Apply the same full-expression borrow rule used by ordinary calls to new, this, and base. Keep fresh owned strings alive through a hidden owner, allow process-lifetime literals, and retain escape rejection for borrowed parameters.

---

>
> EN: Share the G0.2 plan between the GUI and command line, allow selecting parallelism, and return automation-friendly exit codes. Preserve the complete matrix, historical reports, and Gate 0 certification from the existing runner.

## 0087. `0a2d558cd` — Define la política local de historial de desarrollo / Define the local development history policy

>

## 0088. `c209ea0c3` — Fix enum constants, constructor initialization, and native string semantics

- Commit: `c209ea0c3cb5db783ad3a238a228d42b3e3abe47`
- Fecha: 2026-09-08
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Support compile-time enum member expressions, including bitwise combinations and shift-based values, while preserving overflow and underlying-type validation.
>
> Allow simple assignments to get-only auto-properties inside their declaring instance constructor without synthesizing a setter. Keep writes from ordinary methods and through other instances rejected.
>
> Allow equality and inequality comparisons between enums and the integer literal zero while keeping nonzero integer comparisons invalid.
>
> Use the existing string-literal-to-native-string conversion when joining conditional-expression branches, preserving lazy evaluation and rejecting unrelated types.
>
> Allow process-lifetime string literals and fresh owned string temporaries to cross explicit borrowed-string method and constructor parameters. Materialize hidden owners for constructor arguments and destroy them at the end of the full expression, including exceptional exits. Preserve escape analysis so borrowed constructor parameters cannot be stored in longer-lived fields.
>
> Add focused semantic and negative tests, O0/O2 execution coverage, LLD and NSLink coverage, and corresponding specification and conformance updates.

## 0096. `e84ca319b` — Corrige descriptores y API precompilada / Fix precompiled descriptors and API

- Fecha: 2026-09-09
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

Evita además que imports extern C no exportados se reconstruyan como métodos NativeSharp inexistentes. Agrega regresiones de bibliotecas estáticas, DLL, Object implícito y dependencias nativas privadas.
>
> EN: Resolve precompiled class bases through PE-safe accessors and publish the chain with cmpxchg when a local class inherits from NativeSharp.Core.Object or another external type. Also prevent non-exported extern C imports from being reconstructed as nonexistent NativeSharp methods. Add regressions for static libraries, DLLs, the implicit Object root, and private native dependencies.

## 0146. `051581884` — Corrige especializaciones genericas cerradas en NSMETA / Fix closed generic specializations in NSMETA

- Commit: `051581884d90541c895f462feda577f3172a0cae`
- Fecha: 2026-09-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

>
> EN: Publish external specializations used by public contracts, preserve canonical arguments, rematerialize closed types when consuming libraries, and reject incomplete NSMETA. Add a source-free producer/consumer regression.

## 0147. `551bf7e35` — Conserva simbolos fisicos precompilados / Preserve precompiled physical symbols

- Commit: `551bf7e35900f41b4ba2044f3c0f51b2d04857f0`
- Fecha: 2026-09-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Transporta el mangledName de NSMETA al miembro sintetico, canonicaliza solo su asociacion semantica y hace que HIR use la identidad publicada. Agrega regresion con un metodo que recibe un generico cerrado del productor.
>
> EN: Carry the NSMETA mangledName into the synthetic member, canonicalize only its semantic association, and make HIR use the published identity. Add a regression for a method receiving a producer-owned closed generic.

---

## 0148. `c8980c325` — Deduplica genericos con COMDAT / Deduplicate generics with COMDAT

- Commit: `c8980c325dae04c80d2a8b8dfd4b70204107cc7f`
- Fecha: 2026-09-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Propaga la procedencia generica a las entidades sintetizadas y emite cada especializacion como un grupo weak_odr con lider COMDAT estable. Evita duplicados estaticos y conserva exports DLL.
>
> EN: Propagate generic provenance to synthesized entities and emit each specialization as a weak_odr group with a stable COMDAT leader. Prevent static duplicates while preserving DLL exports.

---

## 0149. `7b45956b5` — Empaqueta solo runtimes alcanzados / Package only reached runtimes

- Commit: `7b45956b5e066a250e8aa94ee0f27e6d19825801`
- Fecha: 2026-09-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Conserva la validacion del triplete precompilado, pero activa una DLL solo cuando el MIR contiene una llamada real a un simbolo NativeSharp o un export C del productor. Evita exigir runtimes para imports extern C extranjeros reconstruidos.
>
> EN: Preserve full precompiled triplet validation, but activate a DLL only when MIR contains an actual call to a producer NativeSharp symbol or C export. Avoid requiring runtimes for reconstructed foreign extern C imports.

---

## 0150. `eeb9597e7` — Deduplica runtimes entre rutas / Deduplicate runtimes across paths

- Commit: `eeb9597e787d05b185f172cb0970044a66c01a26`
- Fecha: 2026-09-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Compara el contenido de runtimes homonimos encontrados en rutas diferentes, tanto en referencias directas como en la clausura transitiva. Acepta copias byte-identicas y conserva el rechazo de DLLs distintas.
>
> EN: Compare contents of same-named runtimes found at different paths for both direct references and transitive closure. Accept byte-identical copies while preserving rejection of different DLLs.

---

## 0151. `790917c7c` — Valida contratos precompilados desde HIR / Validate precompiled contracts from HIR

- Commit: `790917c7c4bff9e4397b9564f2b10daecf1e4981`
- Fecha: 2026-09-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Ejecuta la validacion comun de NSMETA, archivo COFF y runtime opcional durante las comprobaciones HIR y posteriores. Evita falsos positivos que antes solo se descubrían durante la emision.
>
> EN: Run shared NSMETA, COFF archive, and optional runtime validation during HIR and later checks. Prevent false positives previously discovered only during emission.

---

## 0152. `8749fcd9a` — Separa COMDAT por simbolo generico / Split COMDAT by generic symbol

- Commit: `8749fcd9a62936d20c7640b670bf11c031104b36`
- Fecha: 2026-09-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Deriva cada grupo COMDAT del nombre fisico de su funcion o global en lugar de compartir uno para toda la especializacion. Permite materializacion parcial sin descartar miembros todavia referenciados.
>
> EN: Derive each COMDAT group from its physical function or global name instead of sharing one across the specialization. Allow partial materialization without discarding still-referenced members.

---

## 0153. `e2c6457be` — Reconstruye interfaces NSMETA por contenedor / Rebuild NSMETA interfaces by container

- Commit: `e2c6457be88f774aaf49efc4d022a1dae67b6b44`
- Fecha: 2026-09-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Emite propiedades e indexadores de interfaces como contratos sin copiar modificadores de implementacion desde sus miembros. La materializacion deja de depender de kAbstract y conserva clases y structs.
>
> EN: Emit interface properties and indexers as contracts without copying implementation modifiers from members. Materialization no longer depends on kAbstract while preserving classes and structs.

---

## 0154. `7e9028ddc` — Reconoce tipos calificados en NSMETA / Recognize qualified NSMETA types

- Commit: `7e9028ddc4b0772ec59b7b0b373fd53be01137e1`
- Fecha: 2026-09-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Usa deteccion calificada en las ocho posiciones declarativas equivalentes, muestra todos los diagnosticos de la fuente publica reconstruida y valida primero los artefactos fisicos.
>
> EN: Use qualified-type detection at all eight equivalent declaration sites, show every diagnostic from rebuilt public source, and validate physical artifacts first.

---

## 0155. `e72823d2b` — Respeta contenedores en simbolos NSMETA / Honor containers in NSMETA symbols

- Commit: `e72823d2b37a18a1ab88ca3662a80c5fbb2d7ae5`
- Fecha: 2026-09-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Excluye de la superficie fisica requerida los campos publicos declarados dentro de tipos internos o no alcanzables publicamente. Conserva campos de contenedores publicos y exports C.
>
> EN: Exclude public fields declared inside internal or publicly unreachable types from the required physical surface. Preserve fields of public containers and C exports.

---

## 0156. `42bf6f5ac` — Publica firmas con interfaces en NSMETA / Publish interface signatures in NSMETA

- Commit: `42bf6f5ac8c007b966d390bf1c852e6c5a9fb9c3`
- Fecha: 2026-09-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Reconoce interfaces públicas y públicamente alcanzables como referencias materializables en parámetros y retornos. Alinea la superficie requerida por NSMETA con los exports de DLL y añade una regresión para ambos sentidos de la firma.
>
> EN: Recognize public, publicly reachable interfaces as materializable references in parameter and return positions. Align the NSMETA required surface with DLL exports and add regression coverage for both sides of the signature.

---

## 0157. `7411b66b4` — Publica arreglos de referencias en NSMETA / Publish reference arrays in NSMETA

- Commit: `7411b66b42834fcbc26c4f9a5262daed806349ff`
- Fecha: 2026-09-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Considera materializables los arreglos de clases e interfaces públicas en parámetros, retornos y propiedades. Alinea los símbolos requeridos por NSMETA con los exports PE y añade una regresión DLL para toda la categoría.
>
> EN: Treat arrays of public classes and interfaces as materializable in parameters, returns, and properties. Align NSMETA required symbols with PE exports and add a DLL regression for the complete category.

---

## 0158. `4fc8329f7` — Conserva tipos nominales en callables NSMETA / Preserve nominal types in NSMETA callables

- Commit: `4fc8329f7b0c4a7254eaf5ff677284b9406ab4f2`
- Fecha: 2026-09-11
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Elimina imports sintéticos que podían volver a enlazar tipos globales con homónimos de otras bibliotecas al reconstruir APIs precompiladas. Rechaza además la emisión de callables públicos concretos sin identidad física y añade una regresión productor/consumidor con dos enums Keys distintos.
>
> EN: Remove synthetic imports that could rebind global types to same-named types from other libraries while rebuilding precompiled APIs. Also reject concrete public callables without a physical identity and add a producer/consumer regression with two distinct Keys enums.

---

## 0159. `bb7295f6b` — Materializa APIs completas de genericos cerrados / Materialize complete closed-generic APIs

- Commit: `bb7295f6bb3f91199c2c854cad9b66fa011f34b9`
- Fecha: 2026-09-11
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Genera todos los metodos publicos y protegidos de especializaciones cerradas publicadas por bibliotecas, incluyendo accessors, estaticos y dependencias privadas transitivas. Conserva la demanda por uso en ejecutables, acumula todas las identidades fisicas faltantes y anade una regresion productor/consumidor basada en ReferenceList<IPhysicsObject>.
>
> EN: Generate every public and protected method of closed specializations published by libraries, including accessors, statics, and transitive private dependencies. Preserve use-driven emission for executables, aggregate all missing physical identities, and add a producer/consumer regression based on ReferenceList<IPhysicsObject>.

---

## 0160. `7cac14b01` — Declara dependencias nominales externas en NSMETA / Declare external nominal dependencies in NSMETA

- Commit: `7cac14b01296d9c81bfbe3e6900f71e57a290fe0`
- Fecha: 2026-09-11
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Agrega a NSMETA 1.12 una tabla determinista de tipos propiedad de otras LibraryReference, evita republicar especializaciones precompiladas y valida la clausura completa contra el conjunto de modulos antes de reconstruir APIs. Cubre los siete EventHandler cerrados y Mat4, Quat, Vec2, Vec3 y Vec4 con un caso positivo y un diagnostico acumulativo cuando falta el proveedor.
>
> EN: Add a deterministic table of types owned by other LibraryReferences to NSMETA 1.12, avoid republishing precompiled specializations, and validate the complete module set before rebuilding APIs. Cover the seven closed EventHandler types plus Mat4, Quat, Vec2, Vec3, and Vec4 with a positive case and an accumulated missing-provider diagnostic.

---

## 0161. `a2c44ad12` — Conserva tipos calificados al reconstruir NSMETA / Preserve qualified types while rebuilding NSMETA

- Commit: `a2c44ad127367b9421673af4c0c89b943baf155c`
- Fecha: 2026-09-11
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Evita recortar namespaces en eventos y operadores de conversión reconstruidos desde NSMETA. Cubre las siete especializaciones de EventHandler y los cinco tipos matemáticos externos con una regresión que usa eventos y conversiones reales, sin cambiar el formato ni las reglas del lenguaje.\n\nStop stripping namespaces from events and conversion operators rebuilt from NSMETA. Cover the seven EventHandler specializations and five external math types with a regression using actual events and conversions, without changing the format or language rules.

---

## 0162. `dc9d6b658` — Delega la destrucción de bases precompiladas / Delegate destruction of precompiled bases

- Commit: `dc9d6b658e65037328b16c9443cd22d1ebcc2630`
- Fecha: 2026-09-11
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Separa la cadena de destrucción en tramos local y precompilado. Ejecuta destructores y campos owned locales, delega el resto a la entrada almacenada en el descriptor de la primera base externa y evita exigir cuerpos OOP HIR que pertenecen al productor.\n\nEN: Split the destruction chain into local and precompiled segments. Run local destructors and owned fields, delegate the remainder to the entry stored in the first external base descriptor, and stop requiring OOP HIR bodies owned by the producer.

---

## 0163. `1b8668137` — Usa el símbolo físico como líder COMDAT COFF / Use the physical symbol as the COFF COMDAT leader

- Commit: `1b86681370207810b01f3b1245934016fa6781e5`
- Fecha: 2026-09-11
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Elimina los líderes globales artificiales de los COMDAT genéricos y usa cada función o global físico como clave de su propio grupo. Conserva deduplicación por símbolo y permite asociar directamente .xdata y .pdata sin secciones unwind huérfanas para NSLink.\n\nEN: Remove artificial global leaders from generic COMDAT groups and use each physical function or global as its own group key. Preserve per-symbol deduplication while allowing .xdata and .pdata to associate directly without orphan unwind sections for NSLink.

---

## 0164. `c69c3fffa` — Entrega a NSLink el contrato de librerías precompiladas / Hand off the precompiled-library contract to NSLink

- Commit: `c69c3fffa6e46b2148bf490a1ab801af76bea74d`
- Fecha: 2026-09-11
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Documenta el defecto asociativo NSL1025, separa los cambios internos de NSC de las obligaciones COFF/PE y define un barrido acumulativo de COMDAT, weak externals, archives, GC, DLL y unwind para evitar resolver incompatibilidades una por una.\n\nEN: Document the associative NSL1025 defect, separate internal NSC changes from COFF/PE linker obligations, and define an accumulated COMDAT, weak-external, archive, GC, DLL, and unwind sweep to avoid resolving incompatibilities one at a time.

---

## 0165. `feedf7c1d` — Mantiene locales los filtros EH tipados / Keep typed EH filters module-local

- Commit: `feedf7c1d05c73a6bc0243d001df38f1b512fbe9`
- Fecha: 2026-09-11
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Emite cada __nativesharp_eh_filter_type_* con linkage interno porque sólo lo referencia la tabla EH de su propio módulo. Evita definiciones fuertes duplicadas entre productor precompilado y consumidor sin añadir otro contrato COMDAT público.\n\nEN: Emit every __nativesharp_eh_filter_type_* with internal linkage because it is referenced only by its module's own EH table. Prevent duplicate strong definitions between a precompiled producer and consumer without adding another public COMDAT contract.

> EN: Emit synthesized implicit constructors as weak_odr definitions with per-symbol COMDAT, preventing a source dependency and a precompiled library from defining Object() twice. Keep written constructors strong and add lowering coverage for both policies.

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Agrega --progress con límites de fase transmitidos y vaciados inmediatamente, duración por fase y avance de archivos durante lectura, lexer y parser. Serializa la salida generada por workers paralelos y mantiene --time como informe acumulativo independiente.
>
> EN: Add --progress with streamed and immediately flushed phase boundaries, per-phase elapsed time, and file progress during reading, lexing, and parsing. Serialize output produced by parallel workers and keep --time as an independent accumulated report.

---

## 0292. `2aa327fc1` — Agrega proyectos fachada sin redeclarar módulos / Add project facades without redeclaring modules

- Commit: `2aa327fc159eb341a541b56b83c212a303463274`
- Fecha: 2026-09-14
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Normaliza la propiedad de fuentes a través de ProjectReference para que un proyecto fachada no vuelva a absorber archivos ya poseídos por dependencias transitivas. Permite fachadas sin fuentes, conserva fuentes propias y verifica el flujo A→U→C completo hasta OOP MIR sin tipos ambiguos.
>
> EN: Normalize source ownership across ProjectReference so a facade project cannot reabsorb files already owned by transitive dependencies. Allow source-less facades, preserve their own sources, and verify the complete A→U→C flow through OOP MIR without ambiguous types.

---

## 0293. `7ff0f94c2` — Documenta el handover portátil de development / Document the portable development handover

- Commit: `7ff0f94c23479790ca658c440d8ee50d9de5613c`
- Fecha: 2026-09-14
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ES: Consolida el estado de development, milestones, entregas posteriores a tanda 5, compilador instalado, evidencia focalizada, pendientes y reglas operativas para reanudar el trabajo en otra laptop sin perder contexto ni ejecutar un baseline.
>
> EN: Consolidate the development state, milestones, post-batch-5 deliveries, installed compiler, focused evidence, pending work, and operating rules so development can resume on another laptop without losing context or running a baseline.

---

## 0449. `b8e5eedb5` — selfhost(tanda-27): expand the deterministic source graph

- Commit: `b8e5eedb5aca6ff58da6fd00664eea5720ecc37f`
- Fecha: 2026-09-20
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> SH25 replaces single-file project discovery with a deterministic graph over project references and compile includes.
>
> SourceGraph adds canonical post-order project traversal, declaration-ordered includes, ordinal path ordering, deduplication, cycle detection, and owner-relative diagnostic paths. The driver exposes --dump-source-graph and verifies all 22 root compiler units with separate FileIds; cross-file semantics remains assigned to SH26.
>
> The directed O0-O3 gate passes with 92 graph units, 22 checked root units, zero failures, and byte-identical dumps. The full accumulated baseline is intentionally deferred until Claude finishes its remaining batches.
>
> Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

- Commit: `7348f4d03e803ca1d671c538e98b7d06f36e9848`
- Fecha: 2026-09-20
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> SH26 gives the 23-unit root compilation one cross-file symbol identity, stable overload identity and deterministic duplicate diagnostics. It consumes public NSMETA/2 symbol records for binary LibraryReference dependencies without parsing provider sources. The O0-O3 gate proves 1,113 exports, 2,845 cross-unit resolutions, a metadata-only callable, valid overloads and exact duplicate-signature rejection; the cumulative SH25 graph gate also remains green. Full LLD and NSLink baseline remains reserved for the final accumulated sweep. Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

- Commit: `d8bfb54a3cdcc6fc5d750969c47b5b17ab3a293a`
- Fecha: 2026-09-20
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> SH27 replaces first-source project emission with one canonical root-program module, removes the fixed four-argument ceiling, and derives stable COFF names from qualified semantic identity plus nominal function type. The accumulated SH25-SH27 gates pass; the directed O0-O3 matrix executes the three-unit six-argument fixture with both LLD and NSLink and returns 42 on all eight routes. Full baseline remains reserved for the final accumulated sweep.

## 0452. `355fb76da` — selfhost(tanda-30): require validated whole-program IR

- Commit: `355fb76da50109741ce11bcbf1d616475ea149d5`

- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

> SH28 introduces ValidatedIrProgram as the only backend admission object, removes the raw syntax/semantic entry, recursively discovers namespaced functions in MIR, and exposes deterministic whole-project HIR, MIR, CFG, dominance and SSA dumps. SH27 and SH28 pass together; every IR surface is byte-identical across O0-O3 and the dependent LLD/NSLink execution matrix remains green.

---

## 0453. `2cb59fc25` — selfhost(tanda-31): lower static data and verify layouts

- Commit: `2cb59fc25ee14cc47e3a4f458c8cbac7ea07b05b`
- Fecha: 2026-09-20
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> SH29 exposes deterministic project layouts for enums, structs, classes and fields; emits typed scalar globals with constant initialization and stable names; and loads them by semantic symbol. It fixes public-const recognition and switches LLVM object emission to PIC after the gate found invalid ADDR32 image relocations. SH25-SH29 pass together, and all O0-O3 objects use REL32 globals and return 42 through both LLD and NSLink.

---

## 0454. `338393403` — selfhost(tanda-32): complete the semantic object model

- Fecha: 2026-09-20
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> SH30 binds the owning type while analyzing methods and constructors, gives this and base explicit typed lvalue facts, and resolves implicit member names against the current type. The gate locks fields, constructors, properties, indexers and operators across O0-O3 and executes a type-owned static method through LLD and NSLink on all eight routes. Instance functions are explicitly excluded from scalar backend admission instead of being miscompiled without a receiver.

---

## 0928. `a83e359a2` — Preserve escape summaries across callable shards

- Commit: `a83e359a2456096202ac5e64d04fcf98964d095e`
- Fecha: 2026-10-06
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Replace the per-shard O(N) outer escape-summary catalogue with one callable-owned summary. Move that summary into the deterministic coordinator during reduction so Return and Global contracts survive isolated analysis.
>
> Directed evidence: escape summaries 8/8, wave reducer 5/5, method bodies 76/76. Merger clang-tidy passed; the monolithic method-body TU timed out without diagnostics after 150 seconds and is documented.

## 0929. `ad374b717` — Make callable reduction amortized

- Commit: `ad374b7171a5a41ed2085610194a85edcafd2b60`
- Fecha: 2026-10-06
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Stop reserving exact destination size for every callable shard. Vector growth is now amortized while the reducer retains its canonical wave/callable publication order.
>
> The 1024-callable O0 probe reduced j1 merge time from 637196 us to 13158 us and method-bodies from 666370 us to 42334 us. Escape summaries passed 8/8, reducer 5/5, method bodies 76/76, and directed clang-tidy was clean.

---

- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

## 0978. `ade7af37c` — Use official NSC and NSLink for test execution

- Commit: `ade7af37c3a685f4cdbe64b993e721d70b333150`
- Fecha: 2026-10-07
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Route every CTest NativeSharp compiler invocation through NSC_TEST_COMPILER and every NativeSharp link invocation through NSC_NSLINK_EXECUTABLE, defaulting only those two products to C:/native-sharp/bin.
>
> Keep host POST_BUILD verification attached to the freshly built nsc target. Preserve Python, CMake, CTest, Ninja and LLVM resolution through PATH or the existing CMake configuration.
>
> Make NSC Builder preflight, environment, cache identity and directed cases use the same official tools. Delegate the windowed launcher to the canonical implementation and make run_failed_tests.py reconfigure its CTest tree while recording exact tool provenance.
>
> Static validation: git diff --check passed; Python AST parsing passed; test.run listed 47 names without executing tests. Functional tests and baseline were not run.
>
> Documentation: docs/compiler-optimization/TANDA-UNIVERSAL-06-CTEST-OFFICIAL-TOOLS.md

>
> Compare clean legacy and v2 builds under identical Release/Clang/static-runtime settings: v2 reduces wall build time by 38.22%, logical executables by 99.53%, observed processes by 71.69%, aggregate executable-link time by 99.06% and clean disk usage by 98.02%.

> Record the successful 1614.11 second O0 v2 closure plus directed category/case latency. The report explicitly makes no legacy functional-runtime claim because baseline execution remains prohibited.

- Fecha: 2026-10-09
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Purpose: implement the Phase 2 CF-TGT-027 contract without changing compiler product behavior or the historical suite.
>
> Scope: register TF-TGT-209..216 in the consolidated host runner and manifest, add an independent level-by-feature oracle for x64 and ARM64, and add a controller gate that requires the canonical eight-case set.

> Algorithms: all tables and scale traversals are iterative; the 65,536-query case uses constant auxiliary storage and no recursion.
>
> Validation: directed Debug and Release gates passed 8/8; the complete new suite passed 45/45 in both modes before the final formatting-only runner adjustment; clang-tidy reported zero findings; manifest integrity was 346 cases with zero duplicates; product diff was zero.
>
> Exclusions: no NSC, NSLink, historical tests, baseline, coverage, deployment, C: paths, remotes, or six protected preexisting files were used or modified.
>
> Evidence: docs/compiler-testing-reengineering/phase-2/TANDA-01-46-HOST-TARGET-CPU-FEATURE-SUPPORT.md

> Change: register TD-DIA-047..052, link the unchanged renderer into the consolidated host runner, add the exact controller gate, and publish evidence.
>
> Preserved: compiler behavior, historic 684-test suite, six protected changes, C:, remotes, deployment, baseline, and coverage.
>
> Validation: directed 6/6 Debug and Release; complete new suite 57/57 in both modes; controller 51/51; manifest 422 unique cases; clang-tidy clean with one documented unknown-ID suppression; only KERNEL32 imported; zero product files changed.

> Defect: a reused compiler-suite CMake tree could retain NSC_COMPILER_SUITE_BUILD_ID_HEADER from an older Builder 3 revision even when the current configuration selected no explicit override.
>
> Cause: the builder omitted the cache variable when build_id_header was empty, so CMake could distinguish neither an intentional empty selection nor a stale cache entry.
>
> Change: pass -UNSC_COMPILER_SUITE_BUILD_ID_HEADER whenever no explicit header is selected. Preserve automatic identity inference from the currently selected nsc.exe and document the causal boundary, evidence, and pending external validation.
>
> Scope: nscbuilder3.py plus Phase 2 tracking, cursor, and Tanda Validacion 03. No compiler, tests, manifests, protected files, C: deployment, baseline, or remote operation changed.

---

## 1385. `cfaefcee1` — Clear stale optional suite selections

- Commit: `cfaefcee1b8c6872d21e8489a7c373d700f3f776`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Defect: a reused compiler-suite CMake tree could retain an archive executable or comparison NSC after the corresponding Builder 3 field was cleared.
>
> Cause: configure_commands omitted empty optional variables, while both values are CACHE FILEPATH entries and therefore survive omission.
>
> Change: emit the exact CMake -U option for each empty optional selection. Preserve every mandatory tool path, test contract, manifest, product source, and execution policy.
>
> Evidence: the three optional cached selections are now exhaustive: explicit -D when selected and explicit -U when absent. Python syntax and cursor JSON passed static parsing; external configuration remains pending.
>
> Documentation: docs/compiler-testing-reengineering/phase-2/TANDA-VALIDACION-06-SELECCIONES-OPCIONALES-CACHE.md.

---

## 1386. `16750def6` — Fix the BuildSystem root toolchain contract

- Commit: `16750def66d9dfa97631e16abfd5cd1c799a0947`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Defect: C12B-BLD-076 configured fresh root trees without fixing Ninja and the complete Clang/LLVM/LLD/NSLink toolchain. The CTest route forwarded only part of the contract and Builder 3 forwarded only LLVM_DIR.
>
> Cause: run_root_graph accepted an arbitrary partial argument list, allowing CMake to select Visual Studio or residual environment tools.
>
> Change: both entry points now pass Ninja, the make program, build mode, C/C++ compilers, linker, manifest tool, resource compiler, LLVM_DIR, and the selected NSLink. The controller rejects an incomplete producer contract before configuration.
>
> Preserved: all six cases, oracles, product sources, other controllers, historical suite, protected files, C: state, deployment, and remotes.
>
> Validation: Python syntax, cursor JSON, diff integrity, line length, 6/6 case inventory, and 2/2 execution paths audited statically. External configuration remains pending.
>
> Documentation: docs/compiler-testing-reengineering/phase-2/TANDA-VALIDACION-07-TOOLCHAIN-GRAFO-RAIZ.md.

---

## 1387. `7b7e1b4b4` — Block self-host consumers after producer failure

- Commit: `7b7e1b4b4319985950a566ac0e0244366b35f03e`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Defect: a failed self-host fixture producer was rebuilt for every dependent case, multiplying one infrastructure failure across as many as 1,536 concrete variants.\n\nCause: ExecutionEngine cached only successful producers and had no causal terminal state for their consumers.\n\nChange: retain the first producer failure per fixture, mode and optimization; report later consumers as BLOCKED with BLOQUEADO_POR evidence; include BLOCKED in final status, retry selection and GUI rendering.\n\nPreserved: no fixtures, cases, manifests, oracles, compiler sources, historical tests, protected files, C: installations, deployments or remotes were changed.\n\nValidation: Python AST and cursor JSON parse; git diff --check; 5 producer projects, 192 logical consumers, 1,536 variants and 40 producer keys audited statically. Execution remains pending on the user's external run.\n\nEvidence: docs/compiler-testing-reengineering/phase-2/TANDA-VALIDACION-08-BLOQUEO-CAUSAL-SELFHOST.md

---

## 1388. `d82394386` — Discover the compiler test denominator dynamically

- Commit: `d823943863d732f685956e80fe30d385c799161c`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Defect: Builder 3 rejected any coherent compiler catalogue whose revision did not contain exactly 44 components and 9,587 cases.\n\nCause: revision-specific evidence totals were encoded as runtime preconditions after loading the canonical inventories.\n\nChange: validate non-empty sources, unique component and case identities, exact design/allocation parity, component ownership and absence of foreign executable cases; derive all totals from the current canonical files.\n\nPreserved: the 606 library rows remain outside the NSC-only suite through implementation-waves.csv; no case, contract, manifest, oracle, disposition, product source, historical test, protected file, C: path, deployment or remote changed.\n\nValidation: the real audit discovers 44 components, 1,208 contracts, 9,587 cases and 5,695 executable rows with zero unclassified entries; a coherent one-component synthetic catalogue passes and duplicate allocation IDs fail; Python AST, cursor JSON and diff checks pass.\n\nEvidence: docs/compiler-testing-reengineering/phase-2/TANDA-VALIDACION-09-CATALOGO-DINAMICO.md

## 1491. `44fbc582a` — Reject scalar manifest tags at raw gates

- Commit: `44fbc582ab392c55f817ae29dca7c752824227d1`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Defect: BuildSystem, Packaging and self-host raw manifest readers could treat the scalar text known-product-defect as if it were the canonical tag array.
>
> Cause: membership checks consumed untyped JSON values before enforcing the manifest string-array contract.
>
> Fix: add one exact non-empty unique string-array validator and apply it at the three raw manifest validation boundaries. Preserve manifests, classifications, cases, oracles, product code and historical tests.
>
> Evidence: 14/14 focused Python tests, 246/246 controller tests, and live validation of 129 cases across four components including nine known-defect tags. No product build, execution, baseline, C: write, deploy or remote operation.
>
> Documentation: docs/compiler-testing-reengineering/phase-2/TANDA-VALIDACION-112-TAGS-MANIFIESTO-EXACTOS.md

---

## 1492. `24e70076a` — Validate raw manifests with canonical parser

- Commit: `24e70076ab4cbc8f521e7a72148393545b7ac7e0`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Route the seven BuildSystem, Packaging, and self-host raw-manifest consumers through nsc_suite.load_manifest before preserving component-specific raw objects.
>
> Reject the schema forms that local validators previously accepted, preserve canonical identity/order parity, and cover 24 mutations across system and self-host consumers.
>
> Evidence: 16 focused tests, 248 controller tests, and 240 live component cases validated. No product build, execution, baseline, deployment, or protected-file staging.

---

## 1493. `ec0a7a2a6` — Validate bootstrap catalog without coercion

- Commit: `ec0a7a2a6af95366c810a90a1a729660fdf1cac1`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Require the exact eight-field Bootstrap.Infrastructure catalog schema, non-empty strings, and unique non-empty case-id arrays before lineage validation.
>
> Remove all str-based coercions while keeping category and language values extensible and deriving closure from the canonical inventory rather than fixed test counts.
>
> Evidence: 5 focused tests with 28 schema mutations, 252 controller tests, 73 live contracts, 438 live cases, and zero missing producers. No product execution, baseline, deployment, or protected-file staging.

---

## 1494. `ba9faec12` — Close every raw manifest consumer

- Commit: `ba9faec12ef34379888fa086d483de3a395904ca`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Route Host.Source, Host.nsc, and the generic pending-component validator through the canonical component_manifest_rows boundary.
>
> Correct the audited denominator from seven to ten consumers and extend the wiring guard so alternate local variable names cannot remain outside the shared parser.
>
> Evidence: 17 focused tests, 252 controller tests, 294 live projected rows, and no direct central-manifest reads outside the shared boundary. No product execution, baseline, deployment, or protected-file staging.

---

>
> Evidence: 7 focused tests with 24 coercion mutations, 256 controller tests, and 32 live classification rows. No product execution, baseline, deployment, or protected-file staging.

## 1496. `402884f36` — Reject coerced system catalog fields

- Commit: `402884f3637f680b8a47f88a7c0a9f1962898b10`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Require exact JSON strings and string arrays for BuildSystem and Packaging ranges, dispositions, contracts, case lists, and contradiction keys.
>
> Reuse typed catalog case identities for Packaging summaries and consume canonical or CSV identities without redundant conversion.
>
> Evidence: 17 focused tests with 11 system-catalog mutations, 260 controller tests, and 512 live catalog rows. No product execution, baseline, deployment, or protected-file staging.

---

## 1497. `87d49c153` — Reject remaining self-host JSON coercions

- Commit: `87d49c1533faf931cce3b069ccba99cb1432aa95`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Defect: Project, Bootstrap.Infrastructure, Contracts and Documentation gates converted JSON identities or case arrays with str() before validating lineage and contradiction evidence.
>
> Cause: these local consumers did not reuse the exact string and string-array boundaries already used elsewhere in the controller infrastructure.
>
> Change: validate the seven demonstrated coercion sites without changing catalogs, dispositions, denominators or product behavior; add 19 mutation cases over the four live gates and persist Validation 118 evidence.
>
> Validation: 24 focused Python tests and the complete 266-test controller suite pass. No product, baseline, deployment or C: action was executed. The six user-owned files remain unstaged and hash-identical.

---

## 1498. `09a5c59ca` — Reject coerced self-host materialized identities

- Commit: `09a5c59cadf1bff08a8ca7281f29729d6d484afe`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Defect: validate_unique_materialized_ids converted an arbitrary first tuple element with str() before duplicate and denominator checks.
>
> Cause: the shared boundary assumed every producer returned a regex-derived string but did not enforce that invariant itself.
>
> Change: require a non-empty string identity; add four synthetic mutations and run the three live Bootstrap, CodeGen and Documentation consumers over 171 materialized cases.
>
> Validation: 6 focused tests and the complete 268-test controller suite pass. No product, baseline, deployment or C: action was executed; the six user-owned files remain unstaged.

---

## 1499. `2021e9ed8` — Contain self-host catalog producers in checkout

- Commit: `2021e9ed8bac37ea0ffa4bfdf5f22afe5bb387ed`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Defect: Contracts and Bootstrap.Infrastructure accepted any existing file referenced by source_path, including absolute paths and relative traversal outside repository_root.
>
> Cause: both consumers checked existence without proving the resolved producer belonged to the audited checkout.
>
> Change: resolve repository-relative producers through one containment boundary and reject absolute, drive-qualified, escaped or missing paths; add four escape mutations across both live catalogs.
>
> Validation: 13 focused tests and the complete 270-test controller suite pass; 121 live catalog rows and 726 cases retain their producer lineage. No product, baseline, deployment or C: action was executed.

---

## 1500. `28e7bfaae` — Reconcile external Builder 3 reports

- Commit: `28e7bfaaedc25aeefdcc86ac9fa0fa10f81b02b1`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Evidence: five attached reports contain four unique revisions; the last two are byte-identical and stop at the same Host.Linker undefined-symbol set.
>
> Finding: current sources contain the Sema support, warning fix, private ABI shim, product identity and eleven Host.Linker producers introduced by the recorded causal commits.
>
> Checkpoint: Builder 3 catalog audit returns 9,587 cases, 1,208 contracts, 44 components and zero unclassified rows; the 270-test Python controller suite remains green.
>
> Limit: no post-closure product build was executed here, so functional link validation remains PENDING_USER_RUN. No baseline, deployment or C: action occurred.

## 1546. `0d57b8592` — test(selfhost): correct punctuation oracle index

- Commit: `0d57b85922b5473e7743ce85b99baee8f9c2e19e`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ## Scope
>
> - Fix only TSH-00445 in SelfHostLexerFullModule.PunctuationMatrix.
> - Add the TANDA-VALIDACION-164 causal evidence document.
> - Leave the lexer, manifest, Builder 3, TRACKING, and all other cases unchanged.
>
> ## Root cause
>
> The representative punctuation input places Question at token index 8 and Colon at index 9. The oracle queried index 9 for Question, so it returned fixture exit code 47 even though LexPunctuation emitted the correct sequence.
>
> ## Change
>
> - Query KindAt(8) for TokenKind.Question.
> - Preserve all other token-count, token-kind, EOF, and error-count assertions.
>
> ## Validation contract
>
> This commit intentionally precedes execution. Only TSH-00445 Debug/O0 will be rerun afterward with the A: NSC and NSLink toolchain; the immutable Builder 3 history will hold the result.

## 1625. `d6e090ed2` — fix(builder3): restore canonical NSC build invocation

- Commit: `d6e090ed26153c4e72dfefa9ad3c21b9225e60d8`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Builder 3 introduced two arguments that were absent from the established NSC Builder invocation: CMAKE_MAKE_PROGRAM during configure and --config for a single-configuration Ninja build. Remove only those arguments while preserving selected modes, paths, toolchain, versions, dependencies, host linkage, optimization, and all product CMake files.
>
> Static evidence: compared compiler_configure_commands and compiler_build_commands with nscbuilder.py and traced both lines to c9a1c52cef3ab404d8d8ad63c33fe63769ef263e. Per instruction, no configure, build, executable, or test command was run.

---

## 1626. `04c8cf10a` — fix(builder3): restore NSC LLVM root parity

- Commit: `04c8cf10a639d567c0c8912698f266222404e255`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> The functional NSC builder selects one LLVM installation root and derives clang-cl, lld-link, llvm-mt, llvm-rc, static libraries, and LLVM_DIR from that root. Builder 3 instead persisted an independent LLVM_DIR and also derived the host tools from its ancestors, so its NSC build could silently select a different package and toolchain.
>
> Add a separate compiler_llvm_root with explicit override, NSC_LLVM_ROOT, and canonical-root precedence. Route compiler configuration, build validation, and receipts through that root while leaving the existing llvm_dir surface unchanged. Preserve every established compiler argument and the authorized Debug/Release extension.
>
> Restore CMakeLists.txt exactly to the LLVM 23.x gate that preceded 6c4c5a516; that product change had adapted CMake to Builder 3's divergent selection instead of repairing the builder. Static audit covers every configure/build argument, persisted fields, cache inputs, and relevant Git hunks. No configure, build, or executable command was run.

---

## 1628. `9e476f1ea` — Test Sema interned type ownership under sanitizers

- Commit: `9e476f1eafd669c171ce531ee1cfeb4580d9b30d`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Materialize TS-SEMA-077 without changing compiler semantics. The case constructs one owned graph containing a nominal struct, pointer, array, function type, and Owned qualification, then crosses the TypeContext destruction boundary. ASan/LSan remains the authoritative oracle for leaks, duplicate destruction, and invalid lifetime behavior. Keep the case InfrastructurePending and leave manifest/profile integration for a separate infrastructure change. No build or tests were executed by instruction.

## 1630. `2166444fa` — Test exact previous-declaration diagnostics

- Commit: `2166444fa4d883e40a2baa7322d29bd7a815aa7a`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Materialize TS-SEMA-101 through a narrow component-test seam over the existing private helper. Cover a null symbol, a symbol without a declaration, and a symbol with a declaration; require no note for the first two inputs and exactly one PreviousDeclaration note with the product severity and message for the third.
>
> Keep compiler sources, behavior, build wiring, and the active manifest unchanged. Document the scope lock, oracle, lack of runtime validation, and pending manifest integration.

## 1631. `dde0dc14e` — Test C ABI recursive-struct termination

- Commit: `dde0dc14ec42d188a2e57953845ebe0ed6d9b97e`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

## 1635. `91edb4620` — test(source): implement boundary race and ASan contracts

- Commit: `91edb4620cc6d16a37e1c38b24ffdcc953459104`
- Fecha: 2026-10-10
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ## Scope
>
> Implements only TS-SRC-008, TS-SRC-040, TS-SRC-041, and TS-SRC-049 in the existing host suite. Source product code, NSC compilation, CMake, Builder, active manifests, inventories, and unrelated tests remain untouched.
>
> ## Oracles
>
> - TS-SRC-008 owns real content of UINT32_MAX plus one byte and requires SourceFile::size to saturate.
> - TS-SRC-040 truncates after file_size but before read and requires nullopt plus the exact read diagnostic.
> - TS-SRC-041 grows after the sized read but before peek and requires detection without a partial value.
> - TS-SRC-049 retains a SourceFile pointer, forces vector reallocation, and requires an isolated ASan heap-use-after-free report.
>
> ## Isolation
>
> The race cases instantiate the canonical SourceManager.cpp body as a test-only peer under a thread-local stream facade; no product seam or copied read algorithm is introduced. The lifetime probe launches the same runner as a bounded child, captures diagnostics, suppresses recursion with an environment marker, and is Required only in an ASan build.
>
> ## Profiles and integration
>
> The greater-than-4-GiB case remains WEEKLY, coordinated file races remain NIGHTLY, and the ASan case remains InfrastructurePending in ordinary builds. Versioned ASan profile and manifest/inventory reconciliation are explicit follow-up integration work.
>
> ## Validation
>
> Static source review and git diff --check only. Per scope lock, no compiler build, runner build, or test execution was performed.

## 1686. `2824cef1c` — fix(builder3): imponer una única raíz LLVM 23

- Commit: `2824cef1c3ef3ae6630e65f56811e546689780b5`
- Fecha: 2026-10-11
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ## Causa
>
> La configuración persistida permitía que compiler_llvm_root apuntara a C:\clang-llvm 23.1.0 mientras llvm_dir apuntaba a MSYS LLVM 22.1.8. La ejecución de BuildSystem daba prioridad al segundo valor y mezclaba productores 22 con LLVMConfig 23.
>
> ## Contrato
>
> - Selecciona una sola raíz explícita, con C:\clang-llvm como único valor predeterminado.
> - Deriva clang-cl, lld-link, llvm-rc, llvm-mt y LLVM_DIR exclusivamente de esa raíz.
> - Elimina PATH, variables de entorno y fallbacks LLVM alternos.
> - Valida existencia, identidad, raíz común y versión major 23 antes de CMake.
> - Publica raíz, versiones y rutas en evidencia y autodetección de UI.
> - Replica el preflight en la superficie modular.
>
> ## Validación
>
> - AST de los tres archivos principales.
> - 5 pruebas de contrato: 23 aceptado; 22, mezcla y ausencia rechazados; configuración antigua normalizada.
> - 6 pruebas existentes de comandos Builder3.
> - Preflight real de C:\clang-llvm 23.1.0 para rutas monolítica y modular.
>
> No construye NSC ni ejecuta la batería de pruebas.

## 1697. `45bb0fd35` — fix(nsc-builder): isolate missing LLVM oracle

- Commit: `45bb0fd35b0fb54556edda1e1eae726fac3f7f25`
- Fecha: 2026-10-11
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> Defect: T12B-00453 removed LLVM_DIR but relied on CMAKE_DISABLE_FIND_PACKAGE_LLVM, which the root project intentionally forces back to FALSE; host package discovery therefore found the locked LLVM 23 installation and invalidated the negative oracle.
>
> Correction: use an empty per-case package root, clear inherited LLVM and CMake package hints, force package-only root discovery, disable user and system package registries plus environment/system search sources, and unset cached LLVM_DIR. Accept the case only when its single configure command fails with the canonical missing-LLVM diagnostic.
>
> Preserved behavior: other BuildSystem cases retain the coherent LLVM 23 toolchain and no root CMake, compiler, or legacy Builder code changes.
>
> Validation: C:\python\python.exe -m unittest nsc-builder.tests.test_suite_preparation nsc-builder.tests.test_diagnosed_regressions

## 1700. `357109aed` — fix(builder3): enforce a global preparation barrier

- Commit: `357109aedffa72b2fac0422391af509a0cecb332`
- Fecha: 2026-10-11
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ## Why
>
> Producer preparation previously closed failed consumers as synthetic NOT_RUN rows while dispatching cases backed by other producers. That mixed preparation failure with test outcomes, produced thousands of derivative rows, and allowed CASE_STARTED before the complete selected producer set was ready.
>
> ## State machine
>
> 1. validate and persist the compiler/toolchain receipt
> 2. plan producer requirements from canonical variants
> 3. configure missing test trees
> 4. build producers one at a time in deterministic order and stop on the first cause
> 5. validate every required artifact
> 6. emit PREPARATION_FINISHED before the first CASE_STARTED
>
> ## Durable contract
>
> - add canonical PreparationCommand and PreparationFailure records
> - persist one PREPARATION_FAILED or CANCELLED cause with command/log evidence
> - store zero case rows when preparation never reached dispatch
> - validate history has no CASE_STARTED and restore the full selection for retry
> - report the run-level cause independently of case failures
>
> ## GUI
>
> - keep rows LISTO at 0 percent when preparation fails
> - show phase, producer, command, evidence, and global cause
> - add Reintentar preparación/corrida while preserving ordinary failure retry
> - move tool receipt validation into the durable worker stage
>
> ## Validation
>
> - compileall passes
> - 42 infrastructure unit tests pass
> - GUI smoke passes without entering the main loop
> - the suite battery was not executed

---

## 1706. `aba24cce8` — fix(mir): lower conditional expressions

- Commit: `aba24cce88097a42615d19c284db3e4f08cf849e`
- Fecha: 2026-10-11
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ## Cause
>
> HIR already represents verified ternary expressions, but MirBuilder had no Conditional dispatch and rejected every such expression before MIR materialization.
>
> ## Change
>
> Lower the condition into explicit true/false blocks, define one pre-SSA result on each branch with Copy, and join through a deterministic merge block. This matches the existing short-circuit representation so the SSA phase can form the merge value without evaluating both arms.
>
> ## Scope
>
> Only the component MIR builder changes. Tests, CMake, Builder tooling, and the project/OOP compiler pipeline are untouched.

## 1708. `3bd89c1fd` — fix(sema): compare strings with null by identity

- Commit: `3bd89c1fd20a870fffd835fa782715907df3e47a`
- Fecha: 2026-10-11
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ## Cause
>
> The string intrinsic branch ran before the general null-literal rule. A guard such as text == null therefore bound StringEquals and could dereference null instead of checking reference identity.
>
> ## Correction
>
> Detect a null literal before string intrinsic binding. Both == and !=, in either operand order, now follow the existing reference-comparison path. Ordinary string/string equality still binds the content intrinsic.
>
> ## Focused verification
>
> The Stage41 fixture covers all four string/null spellings plus an ordinary string/string comparison. The LLVM 23 unit target rebuilt successfully and reported PASS for 79 checks.

## 1709. `c7abf91fc` — feat(core): add direct numeric Parse facades

- Commit: `c7abf91fc267264203d1a2710cfca19bb2a8f865`
- Fecha: 2026-10-11
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ## Public contract
>
> Expose Byte, SByte, Int16, UInt16, Int32, UInt32, Int64, UInt64, Float and Double facades. Each provides Parse(text), Parse(text, fallback) and TryParse(text, out value).
>
> ## Semantics
>
> - Parse(text) terminates explicitly through panic when parsing fails
> - the fallback overload returns the caller value without throwing
> - TryParse remains bool/out
> - malformed and null input share the same non-throwing failure in Number
> - all facades delegate to the single existing Number core
>
> ## Focused verification
>
> Rebuilt nsc with LLVM 23.1.0, compiled only c21_number_parsing using the generated runtime library, and observed the contractual exit code 42. The matrix covers all ten valid direct conversions, all ten fallbacks and null-safe fallback/TryParse paths.

## 1729. `e80cb6ef9` — fix(parser): retain inclusive range patterns

- Commit: `e80cb6ef958aa3e270724f9ce8dac1ed5341fdfa`
- Fecha: 2026-10-12
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ## Cause
>
> Pattern parsing recognized only the half-open .. delimiter. ..= remained unconsumed, so the upper endpoint and inclusive comparison never entered the pattern model.
>
> ## Implementation
>
> - consume both range delimiters and retain their exact spelling in the existing Pattern representation

> - lower inclusive patterns with LessEqual and exclusive patterns with Less

## 1731. `cd01ca7d2` — fix(parser): isolate foreach synthetic identities

- Commit: `cd01ca7d2dc232d3cc110b4441d6547a81f45028`
- Fecha: 2026-10-12
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ## Cause
>
> Parser::parseForeachStatement reused the same compiler-owned names for every foreach instance. Sequential and nested loops therefore exposed colliding source, index, enumerator, range, deconstruction, and pattern temporaries.
>
> ## Implementation
>
> - Reserve one unspellable $__foreach_<ordinal> namespace before parsing each loop body.
> - Derive every synthetic local and pattern binding from that namespace.
> - Teach the semantic view-snapshot exemption to recognize the generated source shape without weakening it to user identifiers.
> - Update focused structural expectations for the canonical generated names.
>
> ## Verification
>
> - Recompiled the four affected objects with the exact LLVM 23.1.0 build.ninja commands and relinked without regenerating configuration.
> - TP-PAR-405: KPD PASS (15,387 us), Required PASS (18,201 us).
> - TP-PAR-406: KPD PASS (841 us), Required PASS (928 us).
> - Removed only these two KPD classifications; mechanical union now contains 26 KPD.
> - git diff --check passed for the commit scope.

## 1733. `d966365b5` — fix(parser): preserve omitted range endpoints

- Commit: `d966365b56661959300fcf87537a221d19866677`
- Fecha: 2026-10-12
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ## Cause
>
> parseRangeExpression always parsed a logical-or operand before looking for a range delimiter, so ..end could not represent a missing begin endpoint. It also attempted to parse an end operand at EOF, so egin.. produced a spurious expression/diagnostic instead of a null end.
>
> ## Implementation
>
> - Recognize DotDot and DotDotEqual before parsing the optional begin operand.
> - Treat EOF as a valid terminator for an omitted end endpoint.
> - Split TP-PAR-431 into precise assertions for exclusive, inclusive, omitted-end, and omitted-begin shapes, retaining exact endpoint and diagnostic checks.
>
> ## Verification
>
> - Recompiled ParserProductTestShim and ParserExpressionPrecedenceContinuationModule with the exact LLVM 23.1.0 build.ninja commands and relinked without regeneration.
> - TP-PAR-431: KPD PASS (13,032 us), Required PASS (3,932 us).
> - Removed only TP-PAR-431 from KPD metadata; mechanical union now contains 24 KPD.
> - git diff --check passed for the commit scope.

## 1734. `bbdb3350c` — fix(parser): retain explicit lambda return types

- Commit: `bbdb3350c6b7e170d7e90bbf87a380166306e322`
- Fecha: 2026-10-12
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ## Cause
>
> The lambda recognizer accepted only a direct fat arrow (or generic constraints) after the parameter head, and parseLambdaExpression immediately required =>. The supported -> TypeSyntax => form was therefore routed away from lambda parsing and could not retain its declared return type.
>
> ## Implementation
>
> - Extend lambda lookahead to recognize an explicit return-type segment only when a fat arrow appears before a statement boundary.
> - Support both parenthesized and single-identifier parameter heads without reserving -> as a standalone lambda signal.
> - Parse the TypeSyntax after -> and attach it to the LambdaExpression before parsing the body.
>
> ## Verification
>
> - Recompiled ParserProductTestShim and ParserLambdaContinuationModule with exact LLVM 23.1.0 build.ninja commands and relinked without regeneration.
> - TP-PAR-450: KPD PASS (3,943 us), Required PASS (4,044 us).

## 1735. `731536d57` — fix(parser): bound grouping stack growth

- Commit: `731536d578620b5c4f11390ece91a7d851e050b1`
- Fecha: 2026-10-12
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

> ## Cause
>
> Each grouping parenthesis recursively re-entered the complete expression precedence ladder. At 128 balanced parentheses inside an interpolation hole, the Debug parser exhausted the Windows stack with 0xC00000FD.
>
> ## Test contract
>
> - Replace the artificial depth-32 failure with a real depth-128 probe in a fresh component-test process.
> - Capture the child exit code without an automatic deadline, keeping the parent and suite alive on stack overflow.
> - Verify that tuple construction and grouped precedence remain unchanged alongside the deep case.
>
> ## Implementation
>
> - Detect pure consecutive grouping wrappers before entering the precedence ladder.
> - Parse their contained expression once and consume/widen all wrappers iteratively.
> - Fall back to the existing tuple path whenever a top-level comma appears.
>
> ## Verification
>
> - Before the product fix, the isolated child reproduced exit 3221225725 (0xC00000FD).
> - After the fix, TP-PAR-511 passed as KPD (223,235 us) and Required (220,726 us) at depth 128.
> - Exact LLVM 23.1.0 commands rebuilt only ParserProductTestShim and ParserInterpolationFinalModule; no configuration regeneration.
> - Mechanical KPD union is 22 and git diff --check passed.

## 1747. `ddcf7dd76` — fix(selfhost): recognize the empty triple raw literal

- Commit: `ddcf7dd761f1314ef2e071e28e5709032bdc1b02`
- Fecha: 2026-10-12
- Autor: Alberto Huerta Luna

**Descripción del commit**

>
> Lexer.LexRawString counted all six quotes at end of input as one opening delimiter of width six. It therefore reached EOF without a closing delimiter and emitted Invalid for the canonical empty triple-quoted literal.
